                  4.3 THE DIÁSPORA AND LAS CONEXIONES WITH SPAIN



                   Spain, through professional varieties, contributes as a European country to the busqueda of
                   new artists and architects in the BMC. Aside from the difficulties of communication and
                   expression that the intellectuals experienced from 1939, the formation of the BMC could not be
                   considered a hecho remoto, it was possible to encounter connections between the college and
                   various Spanish figures that formed part of the educational project. This apartado addresses the
                   presence of these Spanish and the connections that are established between the BMC and
                   Spain.




                   ESPAÑOLES EN EL BMC

                   The vinculos are constituted mainly through professionals of the artistic and architectural field, which were brought
                   to the BMC invited to distribute classes in the Spanish market to finalize the Civil War.




                  José Luis Sert is one of the figures linked to the college history. The Spanish architect, reprized
                   by the dictatorial regime of Franco, was incapacitated by the exercise of architecture and was
                   exiled to the USA. Desde su llegada a Norteamérica, dedicó to the teaching at Yale and Harvard,
                   of which he became numbered, in 1953, decano de la Escuela de Diseño. Labor that is
                   compatible with the professional practice of the Town Planning Associates studio, funded
                   together with Paul Lester Weiner, Paul Schulz and engineers like Elwyn E. Seelye47. Between
                  the trips that took place during the years 1943 and 1944 by North American universities like
                   Harvard, Yale or Chicago, included a visit to the BMC. All of them were organized by Walter
                   Gropius and Joseph Hundnut from Harvard, who were both educational and dean founder,
                   respectively. He was at the college in September 1944, where he gave a course in urban
                  planning, followed by his second visit to the institution, after a brief visit in 1940 accompanied by
                   architects Philip Goodwin and Edward Durrell, and he was treated in the chapter. follow the
                  educational architectural program.




                   At the Summer Art Institute of 1944, he served as professor of escultura José de Creeft48,
                   personal friend of José Luis Sert and the person he accompanied at the college49. Creeft
                   extended its estancia until the finals of the year, participating in the community of active
                   management through the courses that imparted and llegando to realize an exhibition of the work
                   of our alumni published to the public50. In addition

                  VÍNCULOS ENTRE EL BLACK MOUNTAIN COLLEGE Y ESPAÑA El

                   BMC tuvo un compromiso firme con las bases de su proyecto educativo y con la búsqueda de recursos y
                   soluciones de su Existencia. However, there is still an attempt to see if it is within the physical limits of the campus
                   and outside the EU. It is the campaign of solidarity to raise funds, on the Christmas of 1937, for Spanish children
                   who were victims of the Civil War, organized through the North American Committee to Aid Spanish Democracy56.
                   This American organization was founded in the 1936 finals in New York, by a series of groups led by American
                  Friends of Spanish Democracy, to support the Republican band in the Civil War and combat fascism. Joined this
                   campaña screening the film on the Spanish Civil War Defense of Madrid, facilitated by the committee propio57.




                   Precisely, this is the object of the series of paintings made by the artist Robert
                   Motherwell58, representative of the abstract expression of North America that, between
                   the years 1949 and 1976, created by Elegías a la República Española. Unos 150 cuadros
                   dedicated to the Civil War that arose from an interest deserted during his estancia in Paris,
                   following the reading of the work of Federico García Lorca and Rafael Alberti and después
                   del contacto con los intelectuales republicanos en el exile59.



                   The work of Federico García Lorca is also presented in the BMC, as an object of study
                   and reference in classes and sizes of writing and literature.
                   In 1948, Charles Olson directed the production and performance, in dance and pantomime,
                   of Llanto by Ignacio Sánchez Mejía60. Wesley Huss61, theater professor, intensified
                   theater activity at the college with Lorca's children, December 1952, and at La Casa de
                   Bernarda Alba. On the other hand, the work of Brecht and Zukovsky. Fue él quien directed
                   the version of Bodas de Sangre, in Ernest

                   Fenollosa62, of Spanish origin, is presented in the classes of Charles Olson, así
                   as a reflection of all of Ezra Pound's work, revealed in large part in its figure63.



                   Between the connections between college and Spain, Spanish appears as an extra language imparted to the
                   educational curriculum. Las classes de idioms estuvieron presentes de sus inicios a modo de asignaturas
                   optativas que freely escogían los alumnos, no siendo required conocimientos de lenguas extranjeras ni el acceso
                   ni en su graduación. We offer French, German, Russian, English, Latin and Spanish classes, as an auxiliary
                   instrument in the process and resolution of our work, as well as to amplify our knowledge and communication
                   skills. Se introducían en los idiomas, no como un fin en si mismo, sino desde de un cercamiento cultural a través
                   de la literatura y relacionado conceptos con otros campos de conocimiento e implicaciones históricas, alejándose
                   de su capacidad exclusivamente grammatical. For example, during the course of 1941-42 a seminar was offered
                   by Miguel de Cervantes in Spanish, although, in German, a seminar was given by Goethe and, in French, by
                   Stendhal, Flaubert and Proust.




                  The language is presented through Frederick Mangold, secretary of the Board of
                  Fellows, who was professor of French and Spanish during the first years of the BMC.
                  During the course of 1936-37, he studied with Lea Portell Vilá, woman of the Cuban
                  historian Herminio Portell Vilá, professor of history65. Classes are offered in
                  alternating years, interspersed with entry and advanced level courses.
                  The first objects of the introductory courses are comprehensive reading and cultural
                  and literary studies of the idiom, some of which come from sencillos or elementary
                  texts. Text messages in Spanish that are also included in the exams where they are
                  written in English translation66.

                  4.4 EVALUACIÓN DEL MÉTODO DOCENTE. EXPERIMENTATION AND PROYECTION




                   This apartado confirms the model proposed by the BMC as a higher education center. Institución arose
                   from the unconformist spirit of one of our funders who wanted to create a new educational path to explore
                   our ideas. We continue to explain the main variables that define your educational model.




                   The BMC should be understood as a place of opportunity impelled by a group of teachers who decided
                   to design an educational project that would transform students into citizens with a clear social compromise,
                   in addition to being guided by their propias of creative solutions into problem solving. . An educational
                   space that is based on this and where we define our project based on a network of professional contacts
                   that we unite and compromise on, and we form at college. A page in white that its main impulsor, John
                   Andrew Rice, began to write, generating a species of microcosmos, so much so that it scaled down as
                   the atmosphere formed into more of the ubicaciones, located in a natural environment alejado of the
                   urban nucleus of Black Mountain .




                   As an educational community it became a welcoming space for various exiled Europeans and their
                   families. Together with Josef and Anni Albers, passed by the college of psychologists Fritz and Anna
                   Moellenhoff77, personal friends of the couple.
                  After visiting them, in 1935, we received the offer to unite as professors of psychology and biology,
                   respectively, bringing us a place to reside together with our friends. A year later, in 1936, she also joined
                   the college, Xanti Schawinsky and her woman Irene, a fashion designer who distributed design and
                   patronage classes. As an alumna, Irene Schawinsky also attended the classes of Josef Albers and Rice.
                   Later, via Moellenhoff, I took Erwin and Gertrude Strauss to the BMC. The pair is vincularized by the
                   project from 1946, providing classes of psychology and music, respectively.




                  A fact that Hitler's power also increased the number of professionals that were forced to abandon Europa
                   to establish itself in the USA. There, some of them, there is a possibility of work in the BMC. The musician
                   and director of the Colonial Opera, Heinrich Jalowetz, offered to work at the college he traveled to New
                  York, accompanied by his woman Joanna and one of her friends. To think that in a first moment he
                  rechaza of his offer, because the body of music teachers was released, finally was contracted in
                   September of 1939 . llegando to discover the Summer Music Institutes and introduce modern European

                  We can establish various concepts in which the college is linked to the plants realized in the finals of the
                   years 80'91 and which reforzó the relationship between the theoretical aspects and the practices in learning.
                   Learning by doing, the generation of knowledge through the transformation of experience, driven by Dewey,
                   and training in heterogeneous groups of people, foundations in cooperation, work in team and life in
                   community. In this sense, personal relationships are an environment in which all of us recognize and respect
                   each other. Another of the fundamental characteristics of the educational model was its size, with a reduced
                   teacher-alumni ratio, from 5 to 10 students per teacher, capable of increasing the intensity of learning,
                  participation and familiarization between them within the group. Please note that you may consider it
                   personal and that it is reinforced by the studio and conviviality in community.

                   incorporated into the college. With them we will designate the contents of the various signatures that we
                  offer. A structure that, naturally, also arises conflicts deriving from the convivencia and the mix of strong
                  personalities, provocative of various episodes throughout the length of its history, as despidos and renuncias,
                   and that ultimately transforms the BMC. Ante las diversas economic difficulties, los problems para contractar
                   profesores residentes que quisieran establecerse en el centro además de distintos retos que surgiendo,

                  4.5 EL JUICIO DE LA CRÍTICA. MITOS Y REALIDADES



                  In the previous chapter, we discussed photography as herein and the medium of
                  transmission of the following images in the institution, allowing you to illustrate the different
                  actions taken in the country. At the same time, we proceed to carry out a review of your
                  legacy through the published texts, to be able to expose the themes that have been
                  processed and the distinct aspects that have contributed to the generation of the BMC site.



                  During the last years when the college was in operation, from 1954 to 1957, it was published
                  in the following issues of The Black Mountain Review which, as explained previously,
                  included a selection of works by authors and artists of the BMC. Sin embargo, from the
                  cierre of our doors and from the diaspora of our members, that continued with our respective
                  professional and educational trays, until the principles of the years 70', existed a period of
                  time in which the BMC quedó at the dark of the numbers of more participants more
                  conocidos, mainly artists and poets. At the end of the 60's, an article published by Gropius,
                  Breuer and Mies van der Rohe in EE.UU97 appears, a text that refers to the influences of
                  the Bauhaus. In these moments, we also appeared published on the guión of Spectodrama,
                  of Play, Life and Illusion,

                   by Schawinsky and an article relating his memories from the Bauhaus and the BMC98.
                   This relationship, between the Bauhaus and the BMC, mainly through Albers, also appeared
                   in the article published in 197299.

                   In the 70's the BMC reappeared from the publication of the first monograph of the college,
                   written by Martin Duberman100, which focused on narrating the history of its community
                   character and of the personal relationships that it encountered. In some cases, it was
                   necessary to uncover people who were really decisive, making it clear that many of the
                   numbers we knew and that were the source of the BMC's mito, had weak connections,
                   without a clear compromise with the center. This book, Black Mountain: An Exploration in
                   Community, is its propia version of our stories and explains our ideas and reflections, as
                   well as a recognized misconception. So, it is clear that the interviews carried out to
                   reconstruct this aspect of the institution, its a valid work and testimony that served as a
                   party point for other investigations101.

                   Subsequently, it published the first memories of a college alumnus, specifically, of the writer and artist
                   Fielding Dawson102, who has a first-person account of his life during his four years in which he formed
                   part (from 1949 to 1953), under the direction by Olson.




                   In parallel, coinciding with the 40th anniversary of its founding, the North Carolina Museum of Art, located
                   in Raleigh, was prepared, between 1972-73,
                   an exhibition at the college and its contribution to the arts in the USA. This exhibition was organized by
                   the BMC Research Project103, directed by the historian Mary Emma Harris104, author of the monograph
                   The Arts at Black Mountain College105, published in 1987 and republished in 2002, which focused on her
                   artistic program. La publication fundamentally responds to the artists that form part of the trayectory of
                   the BMC, including music, writing and other architects, some of the superficial form. A chronological
                   reconstruction of the college through its most influential artists.




                   After 20 years, in 90 years, Dawson re-edited his memories complete with written material, drawings and
                   personal documents.


                   In 1993, 60 years after the college housed its doors, the BMC Museum and Arts Center was inaugurated
                   in the urban center of Asheville, with the aim of preserving its history and recovering its legacy. In this
                   small exhibition hall, we have the retrospective exhibition, Remembering Black Mountain College106,


                   with works of various artists and part of the permanent collection acquired through the years.




                   Practically speaking, the Poetry Revista, Revista Ilustrada de Información Poética107, dedicó parte de un
                  úmero al centro, siendo el primer y casi unico documento en castellano sobre el BMC. The publication
                   was centered on the writers and the poets, which subsequently became known as the Black Mountain
                  Poets, aunque hace una revisión de su historia por medio de sus rectores, hasta llegar a su ultima etapa
                   donde la escritura, la literatura y los poetas fueron determinants.

                   From the current architectural context that we encounter a complete and inspiring reality, in continuous
                   evolution, where the limits of the discipline are redibujan, configuring new modes of understanding and
                   practicing the profession, resulting in adecuado to study in depth the case of architecture in the BMC.
                   Many articles and investigations1 replant the training strategies in terms of creativity and innovation,
                   fomenting the debate between teaching and learning. From what they arise, or more importantly, we
                   return, a priori less academic and more experimental metodologias, very consonant with the vividness of
                   the BMC. Una mirada retrospective al mismo, como modelo de centro universitario de arts liberales,
                   permite extraer algunas aportaciones surgidas de su character interdisciplinar.




                   La interdisciplinariedad is conceived from the origins of the college as part of the teaching and learning
                   method, without trabas nor limitations, and where each student, responsible for his academic course,
                   selects the contents based on the available assignments. Architecture requires the transversality of
                   knowledge of different disciplines to work, enrich the processes and offer satisfactory results. In this way,
                   we live, where information technologies favor interconexión, proliferate multidisciplinary teams and work
                   patterns between professionals from diverse areas, which generate a diversification in the collective
                  creation models of which the architects form part. A function of the structures allows the components to
                   be free to operate, or not, to the projects, tal y as sucedió en el BMC. Structures in many cases are self-
                  organized, self-managed and self-produced, which reflect the interest and receptivity of the participants.
                   In these situations there is a bus on our project propios and the media to ensure their viability.




                  Above all, moreover, it is strictly a material legado, which is based on the foundations of progressive
                  education. A way to hear the creative process from a concrete and intentional activity, tomando as the
                   basis for the interaction between the intervening actors (profesores –presentes and guests– and alumni)
                   and the work themes, freely proposed by anyone from their estates. Unas conditions of party sustained
                   in the democratic organization of the college, giving freedom, action and experience, allowing the alumna
                   to explore different paths and involucrarse in the process of his learning, following the ideas established
                   by John Dewey in his Theory of Learning Experiencial.




                   The BMC must be interpreted as a learning space consistent with its physical scenario (the campus),
                   the group of our members (profesores, alumnos and collaborators) and the established relationships
                   between them, incorporating the organizational structure of the college. The dynamics between all these
                   variables contribute to the production of learning in the center, understanding how a place of confluence
                  between life and study in community, derribando las barreras académicas establecidas. A determining

                  Architecture in BMC

                   The teaching of architecture is structured around the figures of Josef Albers and Ted
                   Dreier as its main impulses. Albers, as director of artistic teaching, and Dreier, as
                   rector, are responsible for the inclusion of architecture in the program offered. And
                   these figures were shown with the BMC from the beginning of its existence and its
                   history with the apoyo and supervision from the exterior of Walter Gropius, who is
                   one of the educational objects of the BMC. We promote activities with numerous
                   architects representing modernity, which invites you to consider and participate in
                   the program in the middle of taller buildings, generally of short duration. Between all
                   of them we have a number of contacts that, with the

                   llegada de professionals del exterior, estimulaba a los studientes modificando la
                   routina diaria y cercando nuevas ways de sens la arquitectura y el urbanismo. In
                   this way, the contact is stable with Gropius maintained over the years and tried to be
                   consolidated through the projects shipped to the campus that did not materialize.



                  For another part, emergen Lawrence Kocher, Charles Godfrey and Molly Gregory,
                  as material actors of their compromise in first persona with the Work Program. The
                  relationship established between these figures transformed a work program, initially
                  created for the maintenance of campus installations and to foment common
                  convivencia, into an adventure with a clear protagonism of architecture.



                  The presence of the architecture in the BMC is always constant and irregular. A
                  weighing of the particularities of the college, with a teaching not structured in
                  academic degrees and which is not qualified for the exercise of the profession, we
                  understand the architecture as an important part of the educational process of the
                  student. Se han establecido distincttas por las que la docencia de la arquitectura
                  fluctúa. A first step, from 1933 to 1938, with a clear influence of the Escuela de la
                  Bauhaus through the work of Albers and Schawinsky. It was one of our major
                  protagonists, from 1939 to 1943, thanks to the presence of Kocher as a resident
                  architect with full dedication.
                  For these years, the teaching has a dual theoretical-practical character that Albers
                  formed part of his teaching preparation and exploration with the material. At these
                  times the architecture can consider the impulse of the college model. In one case,
                  the presence of the architect was limited to the days provided by the professional
                  guests who were invited by them, as well as the distinct actions taken voluntarily by
                  the students in the context of the Work Program. This program is maintained, in a
                  natural way, promoting what is necessary for the learning of the teaching on campus,
                  until it disappears from the Pauline form until it becomes definitive of the BMC.




                  The architecture presents itself as a global participatory activity, in which the alumni
                  is involved in all phases of the process, including management, financing, direction
                  and material production. The construction of architectural projects that renovated the
                  campus installations, driven by the Work Program to accommodate the needs of the
                  center, within its history of economical survival, contributed to the potential of the
                  paper's enjoyment by the architecture within the history of the BMC.

                   Architecture as construction

                  In the approximation to the understanding of the architecture as it is known, the BMC
                  appears as a clear representative, of which the architecture is 'constructed' and is
                  part of the process of the various selected actions. The student achievements that
                  followed as the scenario of the college were realized by the propios mimbros de la
                  community y del entorno artístico de EE.UU. An interdisciplinar group that coordinates
                  to raise the proposed action where their interests reside in the desarrollo de las
                  mismas. Collaborations based on the action and interaction of the various agents
                  who participated in them and presented themselves as an opportunity to explore a
                  multiple and comprehensive reality. These observations are based on the
                  circumstances that arise and the community of the BMC and, of this brand, which
                  meets the flexible and receptive conditions that invite you to experiment, will be
                  understood.

                  The naturalness and characteristics of the various elegible combinations differ in
                  each case and are not comparable. One of those that, possibly, had a major impact,
                  Fuller's geodesic cup, was a totally planned action and in collaboration with his team
                  of traditional students from Chicago. You should hear how a second part of the
                  previous experience fell. We will also consider the understanding of the college as a

                  see as returns and that we supieron convert into sales, as in the case of the Work
                  Program.



                  In relation to the experimental character, we must understand the propio meaning of
                  the speech, perhaps which is used as a common qualifying attribute in all the

                   The legacy of the BMC


                  The BMC was a catalyst for diversity, both personal and ideological, and that, at the
                  length of its existence, it evolved, as did all the vital processes, until it disappeared.
                  From a general optic it can be differentiated from the branded phases by the
                  renuncia, in 1949, of Ted Dreier and Josef Albers. A first step is where the college is
                  a place of understanding, of ideas and of people, much of Europe with direct
                  connections to the Bauhaus in its methodology, although not in addition to its objects,
                  because the BMC was a university center of liberal arts.
                  We establish parallelisms between ambos centers from the contents and methods of
                  integration of artistic activity with real experience, mainly through Anni and Josef
                  Albers and Xanti Schawinsky, but also through the influence of Gropius and the
                  organization of others of job titles at the college. Let us not understand how the bus
                  of a replica of the Bauhaus model, if not as a natural derivative, through these
                  figures, from where they are known and introduced into the North American centers
                  where they are incorporated as docents. A phase in which the compromise of our
                  members takes place in the temporal and personal decision with the project that
                  forms part of it. In the last years of existence, the center lives as the number of
                  alumni and students dwindles, maintaining that it takes into account the circumstances
                  in which the refusal was accepted, in the manner of choice, through the courses of
                  verano organized, which recovers the initial variety offered at this central location
                  and as a recruitment service to acquire new students and professionals.




                  The stable connections between the BMC and the Spanish professionals that form
                  part of it, do not have anything more than to demonstrate the contribution of these
                  figures in the field of new artistic and architectural solutions. The driver is based on
                  the great number of professionals from different areas that, due to political conflicts
                  and the difficulties of expression in other countries of origin, are incapacitated or
                  exiled, encountered in the USA. a possibility of continuing with your roads.



                  El riesgo asumido por el propio centro, través de su organización autónoma,
                  autogestionada y en un entorno certamente aislado, les dotaba de independencia.
                  A structure in which we have the knowledge to be professional, professional and
                  personal, with the educational project and which combines the teaching, the
                  conviviality and the management, which constitutes the existence and the mismo
                  time, the reason for its final outcome, due to the accumulated difficulties and the
                  inviability of maintaining this formula. The college is the result of the combination of
                  various factors: a accumulation of good professionals from various areas of
                  knowledge, a program of jobs for the maintenance of the community and an
                  academic structure which gives the student a complete immersion in the educational
                  experience and itself. graduate when it is prepared. A graduation with an individual
                  title, it can be achieved and evaluated personally at the center, taken into account
                  the structure of courses or graduates from the rest of the university center. Una
                  identity forjada a través de la implicación de sus participants y de la libertad
                  organizativa en la propia comunidad y en la docencia, reforzada, además, con las
                  distintas aportaciones de los profesionales europeos a los que se cogió. The diversity
                  of curricula demonstrated by the college, full of specialized sciences, as well as the
                  fact that each academic course mutates into the function of those interested in
                  learning to bring their experience and knowledge,

                   configured according to your personality. This misma flexibilidad fue también causes
                   of its constant lucha to survive.

                  The BMC must consider a concrete case as an educational model, for the specific
                  variables that form part of its project and identity: the campuses, that lead to the
                  physical scenarios of life and teaching, that form part of its vital history; the intervening
                  agents, a reduced group of professors and students; There are established
                  relationships between all of them, which were materialized in the design of contents
                  and continents. In some ways, the BMC must understand, in fact, as a process in
                  continuous transformation within a context, the campus, has the final of its existence.
                  After the dissolution, our participants continued with their professional and educational
                  projects, without ever having the diffusion of their ideas, especially the propuestas
                  developed in the artistic ambition at the last stage of the college.



                  Throughout this investigation carried out on the architecture of the BMC, we can
                  affirm that it was intended to meet the requirements of the firm compromised with
                  the present. The differences in approximation to the understanding of the experimental
                  and experiential, constitute a contribution applicable to the teaching in the actuality,
                  siendo a route of reflexión and búsqueda abierta a nuevas prácticas inclusives que
                  refuercen la participación de experiencias reales en el entorno académico, aportando
                  resultados a la society. In this new route there is an understanding of architecture as
                  a knowledge, and that it claims to continue through teaching, between other
                  professional paths, arises new preguntas. Questions about educational methodologies
                  that integrate real experience and that can be linked to the practices of the situations.
                  In parallel, it is possible to increase the dissemination of research through participation
                  in architectural and pedagogical conferences and the writing of articles in specialized
                  publications. Ultimately, it is necessary to channel the energy generated along the
                  length of the route that ends, in a continuous effort to ensure that we continue to do
                  so. This is how Olson expressed it with the motive of the BMC boss: “Has one of
                  these wonderful occasions ended up in a final conclusion”

                                                                                  2
                                                                                      .




                  2
                    Lecture by Charles Olson on Modern Poetry, March 26, 1969, in Beloit College, Wisconsin. In this charla
                  reflection on the final of the BMC as a history not confirmed, of which one of the participants continued with
                  their exploration and personal work. For another part, he is responsible for the transfer of historical and
                  document documents to the Archivo Estatal de Carolina del Norte.

                                                                         Proposes the collaboration between different specialists (urbanists,
                                                                         economists, sociologists, historians, etc.) to determine the
                                                                         programs and subsequently collaborates with artists, painters,
                                                                         photographers to create the models.




                                                                         It is organized in the form of taller, conferences and debates that
                                                                         come from plastic materials in models, etc. 3d


                                                                         The course is divided into 3 parts:
                                                                         1_basis teórica. Main points of the modern plane


                                                                         2_investigate the means of expression and visualization of flight
                                                                         problems. Photographs, films, models…


                                                                         3_comunicar ideas al público general.


                                                                         1939- Inhabilitado tras la Guerra Civil. In June I went to the USA.
                                                                         You are currently residing in NYC.

                               SPECTODRAMA: PLAY, LIFE , ILLUSION


                               1936 and 1937

                               XANTI SCHAWINSKY and our alumni.
                               Schawinsky professor from 1936 to 1938


                               Scenic representation based on the concepts related to optics
                               (across the viewpoint), acoustics, architecture, symphony and
                               illusion (metaphysical aspects).



                               Named: Total Experience.

                               Busqueda del intercambio entre las artes y las ciencias por medio
                               del teatro intendo como laboratorio para la experimentación y
                               escenario de la action. (Duberman. P89-
                               90)

                               This scene incorporates music by Kurt Schwitter (Resonate 1922-32.
                               Basic Sound Sonata) and uses the hereditary methodology of Oskar
                               Schlemmer (Bauhaus).

                               Comes apart in 4 parts. Consta de episodes que previamente han
                               esbozado con storyboard y luego se representedan con techniques
                               de improvisation.

                               Enfasis en las relaciones espaciales entre y alrededor de
                               los coerpos.

                               This part has a design and clothing determined by how to hide or
                               reveal the orientation and layout of the performer respecting the
                               proportion and size of the space

                               Brief stories with basic concepts of the theater in different categories:
                               optics, form and color, acoustics, sound, language, music, time,
                               space, architecture, technology and illusion. P69.experimenters



                               Curso denominado internship studies, but it is not included for the
                               preparation in a determined area of the theater, without the general
                               studio of the space, format, color, light, sound, music, movement,
                               time, etc.

                               The classes and studies are based on the scenario to serve as the focus of
                               the illusion, are prepared for the representation of the different sensibilities
                               and training in the recognition of the visual order and to serve as an excellent
                               laboratory for the investigation and illustration of many elements. The study
                               method is through active participation, experimentation, improvisation, self-
                               experiment and public presentation in the college of conclusions (Catalogue
                               1936-37)




                               To introduce the perspective imparted and deep into the classes of
                               geometry.

                               Bauhaus student in 20 years, assistant to Oskar Schlemmer and
                               former director of the Bauhaus design department.



                               Schlemmer's system: observation of play between heads, no
                               comparison, but in relation to the environment, the context, in a
                               major perspective. P68 experimenters.

                               These productions could be considered the germ of which soon
                               became known as “teatro performance”. Unlike the improvisation
                               in the happenings of John Cage, the representations organized
                               by




                               It was presented at the Bauhaus in 1927. Precedent of the total
                               theater and which apuntaba to the interaction and the syphonic
                               effect. The color and the form, the movement and the light, the
                               sound and the words, the gestures and the music, the illustration
                               and the improvisation.

                               It is possible to consider an experimental theater in which
                               concretely Spectodrama shows a method of interchange between
                               arts and sciences, employing the theater as its laboratory. Color,
                               action, movement, light, sound, words, gestures,…in the bus of
                               new ways of expression and communication. How the movement
                               is carried out in the space can be sufficient to express internal
                               emotions. The core of the participants as instruments and
                               instruments that have a relationship with the performative-artistic
                               performance.

                               Interested in fenomenología.

                               Apuesta por el trabajo junto a other profesionales de distintas
                               disciplinas para que las ideas y el conocimiento fluyan, diluyendo
                               las bordereras que nos separan.

                               An abstract scene in which the participants respond to simple
                               forms, with the purpose of the propia vivid experience in the
                               representation, and how it is involved in the process of forming
                               the representation
                               There is no effect on the viewer. (berlin p103)

                               The scenario and no one is used as a practical laboratory.
                               Berlin Catalog p164.

                               The scenario functions simultaneously as representation,
                               demonstration and record of the student's work during the course.
                               Trata la scene/escenario as a complete art piece not as a
                               structure supporting or aparato.

                               PROGRAMA DE ARQUITECTURA//PROGRAMA DE WORK


                               1936 (official date)
                               1940-46
                               1948-49 still exists
                               BMC Committee. Program coordinated by L. Kocher, resident
                               architect.




                               Complete architectural program organized into projects and collective
                               construction tasks, combined with practice and theory.


                               The alumni are involved in all processes, including physical resources,
                               working with their hands, in the organization of the project and in the financing
                               of the projects through the organization of financing campaigns to provide
                               funds for the construction and development of the installations del Lago Eden
                               del BMC.


                               (What will your dollar buy?, conferences, events recaudatorios, camps, etc.)



                               The work program is part of education and includes several different tasks:


                               The construction and adaptation of necessary buildings,
                               Land acquisition,
                               Working on the farm (comida-self-consumer production)
                               Maintenance of buildings and roads
                               Construction of scenarios and furniture
                               Administrative-management work


                               Campaigns that are advertised with the rest of the BMC program on local
                               radio, through the participation of students in a weekly program. (p107,
                               Heather)


                               Aside from the financing campaigns, we obtain 60% of the professor's salary.


                               It was estimated that $25,000 would be spent on collaborative work and the
                               participation of students and professors. (CHESKY SMITH Y SOUTH,p.51)



                               Curso de verano: Building Experience for architectural students (photo folio
                               archive exhibition Berlin).


                               We have an interest in manufactured and industrialized materials during
                               construction.


                               During these years of war, we received a large number of visits from
                               professional architects and designers, who attended conferences and
                               attended the architectural program that supported the BMC.




                               As we weigh the energy of the program, our commitment to war and the cost
                               of materials, most of the missions are prepared and many of the alumni are
                               supported by the war.

                               DON WIGHT (student)

                               group formed in Cambridge by W. Gropius with Norman and Jean Fletcher,

                               Primer was submitted by Noman F. a Board of Fellows recorded on March
                               30, 1946


                               Student Don Wight created a parallel project for small groups with a night
                               zone and a studio to accommodate 6 students. This action will generate an
                               internal debate on the architectural proposals.



                               Finally, there is a subsidy from the FHA program, federal housing authority,
                               which provides a series of buildings, to provide storage and libraries, of
                               industrial construction type, so that the project is abandoned. La edificación
                               del FHA para alojamiento llega enero de 1947 y esperaban otras tres, para
                               almacenes y biblioteca. (BMC Bulletin March 1947 WRA DREIER
                               COLLECTION PC 1956.72).




                               In the act of the Board Of Fellows of May 13, 1946, there is the appointment
                               of 4 temporal buildings by the FHA, 2 edif. There are student dormitories and
                               other 2 apartments (parejas, doubles). At the special meeting of the Board
                               of Fellows of the 200th anniversary of 1946, it was decided to set aside other
                               buildings in the FHA for libraries and other storage and storage facilities,
                               which were ultimately used as a companion (meeting of the Board of Fellows,
                               February 27, 1947).

                               SUPINE DOME
                               (named by Fuller-vista Dubberman)
                               SUMMER 1948

                               BUCKMINSTER FULLER
                               SI SILLMAN, ELAINE DE KOONING, ROGER LOVELACE, JOSEF
                               ALBERS , ALBERT LANIER, PAUL WILLIAMS, WILLIE JOSEPH
                               PATT PASSLOF (student), KENNETH SNELSON (student).


                               Invited by Albers in 1948, he was invited to investigate his investigations into
                               the geodesic cups.
                               Imparte CURSO DE ARQUITECTURA AND INDUSTRIAL ENGINEERING
                               initiated by 2 students


                               Llega in your caravan Dyamxion full of models and models and with its
                               enthusiasm and personality quickly captured fans. Imparte ditintas conferences
                               desde aprovechamiento de cursos de la industria y la innovación militar,
                               geometría,…




                               B. Fuller in his verano course has the object of raising a geodesic cup in
                               collaboration with alumni and fellow professors all. Llega in your caravan with
                               various models where you have explored the structural possibilities of different
                               geometric shapes. It is made with a set of Venetian aluminum sheets that
                               form the structural skeleton.




                               A few aluminum sheets of 2-inch (5cm) Venetian Persian strips that form the
                               structural skeleton with the object of lifting a hemispherical cup of 48 feet
                               (14.4m) in diameter, with an area of 1500m2 and a weight of less than 270
                               libra (122.47kg).




                               B. Fuller will be aware that there is a dimensioning problem and that it will be
                               necessary to duplicate the dimensions of the tires to be able to acquire
                               sufficient strength and increase the weight of the propio


                               The propio fuller denominó: Supine Dome Entrevista Dubberman p14




                               Aerial photo supine dome WRA, HAZEL LARSEN (3JUNIO)


                               This is the first intention to realize collective, participatory and cooperative
                               activities in the BMC community.


                               It is part of an object and it is understood as an experimental process in which
                               the crash is part of the mismo. Tal y como B. Fuller dijo: “You succumb when
                               you stop failing”. It is necessary to make an error to follow the example. This
                               error is part of the process and the experimental method per tb. How it is
                               necessary to assume the riesgo in the educational process, dynamic-action.




                               What is interesting is the completion of a process carried out in a cable as a
                               collective action without taking into account the issue.
                               smashed the result. The important part of the lesson learned

                               through the overall design process.


                               Methodology of work abierta like that of Cage and Cunningham, great friends.
                               In contrast to Albers, more sistematizada.




                               Intention that the students plan the construction of structural manner,
                               cuestionando the principles of structural without accepting the formal
                               conventions of the architecture. Experimenters p121




                               It is also demonstrated that structures can be built gradually, reducing
                               material and economic efficiency during construction.

                               Lindsey Snelson was the first persona who had the basis of the structure plotted

                               geodesic in the Pentagon. In the BMC there is a skin resistant to 6mm vinyl

                               is more likely to be patented.



                               Geodesic investigations that claim to be ideal shelter-refugees at the stage

                               An observation with a repercusión in the context of architecture, which has
                               served as a starting point for various investigations in various architectural and
                               artistic disciplines.

                               THE KINGFISHERS


                               SUMMER 1950

                               BETTY JENNERJAHN , PETER JENNERJAHN and CHARLES OLSON


                               A dramatic representation of the poem by Charles Olson called “The
                               Kingfishers” was realized.


                               “The Kingfishers,” a poem written in 1949 by Olson, was a song about
                               renewal and the possibility of change in the West.




                               Kigfisher (Martin fisherman), was a symbol of renewal in various
                               North American indigenous cultures.

                               First verso:
                               “Lo que no change/es la voluntad de change”
                               “What does not change/is the will to change”
                               You read in the guest room Charles Olson wrote his poem in
                               your voice, Betty Jenner and Pete Jenner played a specific piece for
                               this act on the piano.

                               THE GLYPH


                               SUMMER 1951, July

                               CHARLES OLSON (Poetry),
                               LOU HARRISON (Music), BEN SHAHN (Escenografía y
                               pinturas), KATHERINE LITZ (Danza)


                               Multimedia activity led by Charles Olson based on the roots of poetry
                               in performance.


                               Realize a course of poetry and theater which highlights the importance
                               of spontaneity and interdisciplinary collaboration.



                               Tras la estancia de Olson en Yucatán, 4 meses, vuelve al BMC en
                               verano 51, inspired by the Mayan glifos. Geometric, vegetal and
                               animal shapes painted and tall in stones, glifo, a mode of writing of
                               the Mayan civilization.
                               Olson was fascinated by the double character of the glifo, writing and
                               images.


                               The result consists of a composition of distinct images and disciplines
                               in a single work: poetry-glifo, painting-glifo, dance-glifo. Is the
                               representation of the common idea of glifo expressed by the different
                               artistic forms, a composed image contained in one single object.


                               A gliptico spectacle initiated by Ben Shahn after having dado to
                               Olson a drawing a change of a poem, and a cause of these of
                               the acts, Litz added a number to his repertoire, a Glifo,
                               with one in scene of Shahn and letra
                               by Olson and music by Harrison, composed “A glyph for Kathy
                               Litz”.

                               Katherine Litz defines it as “intercambio de regalos-glifo”.
                               Molesworth, H and Erickson, R, 2015, ob.cit., p.329
                               Interés en la improvisationación del jazz.

                               Back off. Part 1-Olson and Shahn:
                               It is clear that Olson's poem was shared with Shahn. Olson, in July
                               51, worked in various poems and also dance guides: “Apollonius of
                               Tyanna” and “The Born Dancer”, of which he is interested in integrating
                               poetry, dance and music. There are possibilities for the poem entered:
                               The Glyph and “A Round & a Canon, this is the last dedicated to K.
                               Litz and Lou Harrison. (Sg. Vincent Katz, BMC catalogop 186-87)




                               Poem in exhibition catalog in Berlin p.393 and Molesworth, H and
                               Erickson, R, 2015, ob.cit., p.329, poem dedicated to the marriage of
                               Shahn and Alvin, sobrino de los cocineros del BMC, Jack Lipsey:

                THE ARQUITECTURADELACONTE CIMIENTO
                PATTERN CHARACTER IN STICKS




                                              HÁPTICO

                                              Touch visual


                                              The importance of texture and visual touch in the activities and materials
                                              developed in the BMC: art, architecture and performance. Included in the
                                              photography and photo collages by Albers' example.



                                              All materials have a physiological effect on us…. We perceive *material+
                                              qualities partly with the eyes, and speak of 'optical perception' of Material.
                                              But more, and much better, we perceive matter through the fingertips. We
                                              call this 'tactile perception'. We recognize the importance of fingertip feeling.
                                              Today, we have to reconquer this feeling anew, because we have almost
                                              lost it. Albers 1935, in La Habana.


                                              Josef Albers, 'Kombinative Gestaltung' (Combinative Form), lecture
                                              presented at the Lyceum, Havana (2
                                              January 1935). Quoted in Horowitz, 'Albers the Teacher', p. 127




                                              In the Bauhaus tradition, haptic perception was fundamental in its curriculum
                                              and philosophy. Contact physical contact with materials and objects as
                                              fundamental as they are in learning.


                                              Josef and Anni Albers are an example or one of our examples through the
                                              various works that were carried out: photo collage, printed works, drawings,
                                              textiles, joys,….
                                              The importance of the haptic in your work is something you don't think about
                                              to be able to perceive and experience it from the perspective. A feeling of
                                              touch mediatizado by the vision in addition to working in 2D.



                                              Stone textures Quiet House, pillars of the lower studio building, Obras de
                                              Rauscheberg, Albers,…
                                              Textiles by Anni Albers


                                              Models, models, experiments with local materials available,…Materia.



                                              Molesworth, H and Erickson, R, 2015, Leap Before You Look: Black
                                                      Mountain College 1933–1957, Yale University Press in association.
                                                      Institute of Contemporary Art Boston.


                                              Horowitz, FA and Danilowitz, B., 2006, Josef Albers: to open eyes: The Bauhaus, the
                                                     Black Mountain College and Yale, Phaidon, London.

                               JOSE LUIS SERVES


                               Summer 1944


                               Arquitecto español. (1902 Barcelona - 1983 New York.)


                               The first visited the building in December 1940 accompanied by Philip Goodwin,
                               architect with E. Durrell Scott of MoMA in 1939.




                               Then, in 1944, the first courses celebrated by BMC ART INSTITUTE SUMMER
                               1944 were invited as a visiting professor of architecture to their classes.




                               During his stay at the BMC, in September 44, he gave a course on urbanism and
                               three conferences under the titles: “The Human Scale in Planning/Urbanism
                               versus Suburbanism/Civic Centers – Tomorrow”.


                               Estancia: September 11-16, 1944

                               In this verano and in this Art Institute Summer offered in class
                               also:

                               Anni Albers, Joseph Breitenbach, Belle Boas, Jean Charlot, Vistor D`Amico, José

                               de Creeft, Walter Gropius, Barbara Morgan, J. B: Neuman, Amédée Ozenfant,

                               James Prestini, Bernard Rudofsky, Howard Thomas and Josef Albers.




                               Served via Harvard, Yale, Chicago and Black Mountain College between the
                               beginning of 1943 and 1944.
                               In Dreier's letter to Kocher on May 27, 1945, he became interested in being a
                               professor of planning and urban planning with Paul Lester Wiener, sociologist and
                               professional in NY. Kocher will visit them in NY. WRA ncsa, Private Collection
                               Subseries 6, dreier correspondence




                               When the Civil War was reprised by Franco's dictatorial government, it was
                               incapacitated by the exercise of architecture, so in 1939, when he was young, he
                               exiled himself to the United States, New York. In 1941 the North American
                               Nationality was established.




                               He was a professor of architecture at Yale University (1944) and was named dean
                               of the School of Design in 1953.

                               Harvard University, cargo ship that was sent in 1969. Joined Harvard at the
                               invitation of Gropius.


                               In 1941/2? published on text: Can our cities survive? Sobre la ciudad

                               functionalcional. En Harvard University Press que recoge las tesis del CIAM

                               IV. Gropius invited him to a conference cycle at Harvard.




                               In 1945, EE.UU. created together with Paul Lester Weiner, Paul Schulz and
                               engineers like Elwyn E. Seelye an architectural and urban planning studio: Town
                               Planning Associates.




                                                        462

--- Page 227 ---
Machine Translated by Google




                               In the USA its work is centered in the field of urbanism introducing the
                               concept of “URBAN DISEÑO” (also lo llama él). “Urban design” as an
                               extension of architecture, of architectural interests in the
                               problems of major scale, as well as the problems of the city. Hace apología
                               del trabajo en equipo e inclusiveo interdisciplinar.


                               There are 2 team work experiences: GATCPAC, available for team work
                               and HARVARD with Gropius. En Harvard organized in teams in this
                               interrelationship with other departments. Similar experience and work
                               carried out in South America.




                               “Creo much in collective work, creo que se aprende mucho. To the good
                               young people confide it mucha fuerza, that no tenderría sola, no tan solo
                               física sino de mutua motivación”


                               “Another important point in my origins is the relationship between architecture
                               and the arts. Architecture is one of the most beautiful and most complex
                               arts. The city is the mother of the artes. This is the element that allows
                               all art to be established in relationships.” “With enthusiasm for my
                               joys”


                               “Las palabras racional y funcional las he llegado a detestar.
                               Han sido de masiado empleadas y se hacho un culto de todo esto. Todo
                               un dictionario que no nos ayuda en nada.
                               Hay that hacer las cosas as they hacían antes without having labels.
                               Among my words I am not interested as much as the three-dimensional
                               plastic parts have their new form of expression. Our expressions with
                               palabras, but sobretodo con hechos”.

                               Llega has EE.UU. In 1929 and 1940 it was nationalized. Settled in New York.




                               Your work is characterized by direct work on stone and wood, as are the
                               materials based on the work. Your form of work teaches the self-construction
                               of the herramientas propias and the construction of a forja.

                               La Farge,


                               Estudia en España, Francia y Alemania.Emigrata a Estados Unidos en 1936. En

                               Rothko, Barnett Newman and Franz Kline (abstract expressionism), together
                               with those who formed the New York Studio School of Drawing, Painting and
                               Sculpture,

                               ROBERT CREELY


                               1954-57.


                               You temporarily reside in Mallorca.


                               Between 1954 and 1957 he was editor of the Black Mountain Review and
                               professor of literature and writing at BMC.


                               In 1951 R. Creely abandoned EE.UU. He settled in France for economic
                               reasons and interests in European culture.
                               Settled in 1953 (in 1951 by Russell and Cohn) in Mallorca, the Creely family:
                               Robert, my wife Anna McKinnon and three children
                               You live in Marsella. Long live in Bañalbufar and después in Bonova. All
                               coincides with other poets like Martin Seymour-Smith who collaborated on the
                               editorial project. Hasta 1955.




                               From the imprenta Hermanos Mossen Alcover (*Alcovar según Duberman) in
                               Mallorca, print the reviews of BMC: THE BLACK MOUNTAIN REVIEW created by
                               R. Creely based on the suggestions of Charles Olson and poetry books from the
                               editorial project of the owner Robert Creely, written MISCELLANEOUS PRESS.




                               Should this application have the prints (from 500 to 750 copies) of 13?


                               BMC service numbers.


                               In Mallorca he wrote: the novel The Island, and the collection of short
                               stories: The Gold Diggers. In 1954 there were 55 routes between Mallorca
                               and BMC
                               You can print some Origin and BMC Review numbers, so the printing
                               costs are more economical and can be sent to the EU. he stood
                               up. The review is the promotional element that supports your work and
                               intends to collect funds for your activities.

                               THE BLACK MOUNTAIN COLLEGE REVIEW muestra la
                               interdisciplinariedad del BMC, donde aparece Prosa, poesía, cartas, photos,
                               sketches y pinturas. Opportunity to publish unpublished works of poetry and
                               poetry, but also critical texts, ideas, manifestos


                               etc
                               Much of the aportaciones appears as interdisciplinary discussions.


                               The first year was published quarterly and subsequently annually.


                               Funded by Charles Olson and editor Robert Creely.
